+uint64_t computeLocalHash(const std::vector<val_t>& energy,
+                          const std::vector<val_t>& accumulated_flux,
+                          idx_t global_offset) {
+    uint64_t local_hash = 0;
+#pragma omp parallel for reduction(^ : local_hash) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < energy.size(); ++i) {
+        const idx_t global_index = global_offset + i;
+        const uint64_t energy_bits = std::bit_cast<uint64_t>(energy[i]);
+        const uint64_t flux_bits = std::bit_cast<uint64_t>(accumulated_flux[i]);
+        local_hash ^= (energy_bits + global_index) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        local_hash ^= (flux_bits + global_index) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+    }
+    return local_hash;
+}
+
+bool validateResults(const std::vector<val_t>& energy,
+                     const std::vector<val_t>& accumulated_flux,
+                     int rank) {
+    val_t local_energy_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t local_flux_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t local_energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
+    val_t local_energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
+
+#pragma omp parallel for reduction(+ : local_energy_sum, local_flux_sum) \
+    reduction(max : local_energy_max) reduction(min : local_energy_min) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < energy.size(); ++i) {
+        local_energy_sum += energy[i];
+        local_flux_sum += accumulated_flux[i];
+        local_energy_max = std::max(local_energy_max, energy[i]);
+        local_energy_min = std::min(local_energy_min, energy[i]);
+    }
+
+    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t energy_max = 0.0;
+    val_t energy_min = 0.0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_sum, &energy_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_flux_sum, &flux_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_max, &energy_max, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_min, &energy_min, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MIN, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+
+    int valid = 1;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        printf("Validation results:\n");
+        printf("  Energy sum: %.12f\n", energy_sum);
+        printf("  Flux sum: %.2f\n", flux_sum);
+        printf("  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", energy_min, energy_max);
+
+        constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {
+            printf("  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        } else if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {
+            printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {
+            printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
+            printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (valid != 0) {
+            printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    return valid != 0;
+}
+

+void launchRows(const val_t* energy,
+                const val_t* accumulated_flux,
+                val_t* next_energy,
+                val_t* next_accumulated_flux,
+                const val_t* top_halo,
+                const val_t* bottom_halo,

-    return true;
+
+    const size_t elements = static_cast<size_t>(end_local_row - first_local_row) * grid_size;
+    const int blocks = static_cast<int>((elements + kThreadsPerBlock - 1) / kThreadsPerBlock);
+    updateRowsKernel<<<blocks, kThreadsPerBlock, 0, stream>>>(
+        energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux, top_halo, bottom_halo,
+        grid_size, partition.first_row, partition.rows, first_local_row, end_local_row);

-uint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {
-    uint64_t hash = 0;
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {

-        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);

+void runSimulation(const RowPartition& partition,
+                   int grid_size,
+                   int iterations,
+                   int rank,
+                   int ranks,
+                   val_t*& energy,
+                   val_t*& accumulated_flux,
+                   val_t*& next_energy,
+                   val_t*& next_accumulated_flux,
+                   val_t* top_halo,
+                   val_t* bottom_halo,
+                   val_t* send_top,
+                   val_t* send_bottom,
+                   val_t* receive_top,
+                   val_t* receive_bottom,
+                   cudaStream_t compute_stream,
+                   cudaStream_t boundary_stream,
+                   cudaStream_t copy_stream) {
+    const int previous_rank = rank == 0 ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank - 1;
+    const int next_rank = rank + 1 == ranks ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank + 1;
+    const size_t row_bytes = static_cast<size_t>(grid_size) * sizeof(val_t);
+
+    for (int iteration = 0; iteration < iterations; ++iteration) {
+        // Begin the host-staged halo exchange.  Pinned buffers make this work with
+        // both CUDA-aware and conventional MPI implementations without a runtime
+        // dependency on a particular MPI GPU transport.
+        if (previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(send_top, energy, row_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, copy_stream));
+        }
+        if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(send_bottom,
+                                       energy + static_cast<size_t>(partition.rows - 1) * grid_size,
+                                       row_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, copy_stream));
+        }
+
+        // Interior rows do not depend on a halo and run while MPI transfers edges.
+        launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
+                   top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition, 1, partition.rows - 1,
+                   compute_stream);
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(copy_stream));
+
+        MPI_Request requests[4];
+        int request_count = 0;
+        if (previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Irecv(receive_top, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, previous_rank, kBottomHaloTag,
+                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Isend(send_top, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, previous_rank, kTopHaloTag,
+                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
+        }
+        if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Irecv(receive_bottom, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, next_rank, kTopHaloTag,
+                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Isend(send_bottom, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, next_rank, kBottomHaloTag,
+                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
+        }
+        if (request_count != 0) {
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Waitall(request_count, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE));
+        }
+
+        if (previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(top_halo, receive_top, row_bytes,
+                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, copy_stream));
+        }
+        if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(bottom_halo, receive_bottom, row_bytes,
+                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, copy_stream));
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(copy_stream));
+
+        // The boundary stream writes disjoint rows, so it overlaps the remaining
+        // interior work.  A one-row partition has one boundary row, not two.
+        if (partition.rows == 1) {
+            launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
+                       top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition, 0, 1, boundary_stream);
+        } else {
+            launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
+                       top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition, 0, 1, boundary_stream);
+            launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
+                       top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition,
+                       partition.rows - 1, partition.rows, boundary_stream);
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(compute_stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(boundary_stream));
+        std::swap(energy, next_energy);
+        std::swap(accumulated_flux, next_accumulated_flux);
     }
-    return hash;
 }
 
-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
+uint64_t computeLocalHash(const std::vector<val_t>& energy,
+                          const std::vector<val_t>& accumulated_flux,
+                          idx_t global_offset) {
+    uint64_t local_hash = 0;
+#pragma omp parallel for reduction(^ : local_hash) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < energy.size(); ++i) {
+        const idx_t global_index = global_offset + i;
+        const uint64_t energy_bits = std::bit_cast<uint64_t>(energy[i]);
+        const uint64_t flux_bits = std::bit_cast<uint64_t>(accumulated_flux[i]);
+        local_hash ^= (energy_bits + global_index) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        local_hash ^= (flux_bits + global_index) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+    }
+    return local_hash;
+}
+
+bool validateResults(const std::vector<val_t>& energy,
+                     const std::vector<val_t>& accumulated_flux,
+                     int rank) {
+    val_t local_energy_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t local_flux_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t local_energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
+    val_t local_energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
+
+#pragma omp parallel for reduction(+ : local_energy_sum, local_flux_sum) \
+    reduction(max : local_energy_max) reduction(min : local_energy_min) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < energy.size(); ++i) {
+        local_energy_sum += energy[i];
+        local_flux_sum += accumulated_flux[i];
+        local_energy_max = std::max(local_energy_max, energy[i]);
+        local_energy_min = std::min(local_energy_min, energy[i]);
+    }
+
+    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t energy_max = 0.0;
+    val_t energy_min = 0.0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_sum, &energy_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_flux_sum, &flux_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_max, &energy_max, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_min, &energy_min, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MIN, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+
+    int valid = 1;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        printf("Validation results:\n");
+        printf("  Energy sum: %.12f\n", energy_sum);
+        printf("  Flux sum: %.2f\n", flux_sum);
+        printf("  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", energy_min, energy_max);
+
+        constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {
+            printf("  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        } else if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {
+            printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {
+            printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
+            printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (valid != 0) {
+            printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    return valid != 0;
+}
+

+                    const RowPartition process_partition = partitionRows(grid_size, process, ranks);
+                    receive_counts[process] = process_partition.rows * grid_size;
+                    displacements[process] = process_partition.first_row * grid_size;
+                }
+                global_energy.resize(global_elements);
+            }
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Gatherv(host_energy.data(), static_cast<int>(local_elements), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                                  rank == 0 ? global_energy.data() : nullptr,
+                                  rank == 0 ? receive_counts.data() : nullptr,
+                                  rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                                  MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+            if (rank == 0) {
+                print_results(global_energy, "ElementEnergy");
+            }
+        }
+
+        if (validate && !validateResults(host_energy, host_flux, rank)) {
+            exit_code = 1;
+        }
+    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, error.what());
+        exit_code = 1;
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    if (copy_stream != nullptr) cudaStreamDestroy(copy_stream);
+    if (boundary_stream != nullptr) cudaStreamDestroy(boundary_stream);
+    if (compute_stream != nullptr) cudaStreamDestroy(compute_stream);
+    if (receive_bottom != nullptr) cudaFreeHost(receive_bottom);
+    if (receive_top != nullptr) cudaFreeHost(receive_top);
+    if (send_bottom != nullptr) cudaFreeHost(send_bottom);
+    if (send_top != nullptr) cudaFreeHost(send_top);
+    if (bottom_halo != nullptr) cudaFree(bottom_halo);
+    if (top_halo != nullptr) cudaFree(top_halo);
+    if (flux_b != nullptr) cudaFree(flux_b);
+    if (flux_a != nullptr) cudaFree(flux_a);
+    if (energy_b != nullptr) cudaFree(energy_b);
+    if (energy_a != nullptr) cudaFree(energy_a);
+    if (local_comm != MPI_COMM_NULL) MPI_Comm_free(&local_comm);
+
+    int global_exit_code = 0;
+    MPI_Allreduce(&exit_code, &global_exit_code, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return global_exit_code;
 }

+void launchRows(const val_t* energy,
+                const val_t* accumulated_flux,
+                val_t* next_energy,
+                val_t* next_accumulated_flux,
+                const val_t* top_halo,

+        energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux, top_halo, bottom_halo,

-// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification
-uint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {
-    uint64_t hash = 0;
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {
-        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values
-        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);

-        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+void runSimulation(const RowPartition& partition,
+                   int grid_size,
+                   int iterations,
+                   int rank,
+                   int ranks,
+                   val_t*& energy,
+                   val_t*& accumulated_flux,
+                   val_t*& next_energy,
+                   val_t*& next_accumulated_flux,
+                   val_t* top_halo,
+                   val_t* bottom_halo,
+                   val_t* send_top,
+                   val_t* send_bottom,
+                   val_t* receive_top,
+                   val_t* receive_bottom,
+                   cudaStream_t compute_stream,
+                   cudaStream_t boundary_stream,
+                   cudaStream_t copy_stream) {
+    const int previous_rank = rank == 0 ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank - 1;
+    const int next_rank = rank + 1 == ranks ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank + 1;
+    const size_t row_bytes = static_cast<size_t>(grid_size) * sizeof(val_t);
+
+    for (int iteration = 0; iteration < iterations; ++iteration) {
+        // Begin the host-staged halo exchange.  Pinned buffers make this work with
+        // both CUDA-aware and conventional MPI implementations without a runtime
+        // dependency on a particular MPI GPU transport.
+        if (previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(send_top, energy, row_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, copy_stream));
+        }
+        if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(send_bottom,
+                                       energy + static_cast<size_t>(partition.rows - 1) * grid_size,
+                                       row_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, copy_stream));
+        }
+
+        // Interior rows do not depend on a halo and run while MPI transfers edges.
+        launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
+                   top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition, 1, partition.rows - 1,
+                   compute_stream);
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(copy_stream));
+
+        MPI_Request requests[4];
+        int request_count = 0;
+        if (previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Irecv(receive_top, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, previous_rank, kBottomHaloTag,
+                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Isend(send_top, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, previous_rank, kTopHaloTag,
+                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
+        }
+        if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Irecv(receive_bottom, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, next_rank, kTopHaloTag,
+                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Isend(send_bottom, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, next_rank, kBottomHaloTag,
+                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
+        }
+        if (request_count != 0) {
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Waitall(request_count, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE));
+        }
+
+        if (previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(top_halo, receive_top, row_bytes,
+                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, copy_stream));
+        }
+        if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(bottom_halo, receive_bottom, row_bytes,
+                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, copy_stream));
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(copy_stream));
+
+        // The boundary stream writes disjoint rows, so it overlaps the remaining
+        // interior work.  A one-row partition has one boundary row, not two.
+        if (partition.rows == 1) {
+            launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
+                       top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition, 0, 1, boundary_stream);
+        } else {
+            launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
+                       top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition, 0, 1, boundary_stream);
+            launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
+                       top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition,
+                       partition.rows - 1, partition.rows, boundary_stream);
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(compute_stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(boundary_stream));
+        std::swap(energy, next_energy);
+        std::swap(accumulated_flux, next_accumulated_flux);
     }
-    return hash;
 }

-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
+uint64_t computeLocalHash(const std::vector<val_t>& energy,
+                          const std::vector<val_t>& accumulated_flux,
+                          idx_t global_offset) {
+    uint64_t local_hash = 0;
+#pragma omp parallel for reduction(^ : local_hash) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < energy.size(); ++i) {
+        const idx_t global_index = global_offset + i;
+        const uint64_t energy_bits = std::bit_cast<uint64_t>(energy[i]);
+        const uint64_t flux_bits = std::bit_cast<uint64_t>(accumulated_flux[i]);
+        local_hash ^= (energy_bits + global_index) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        local_hash ^= (flux_bits + global_index) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+    }
+    return local_hash;
+}
+
+bool validateResults(const std::vector<val_t>& energy,
+                     const std::vector<val_t>& accumulated_flux,
+                     int rank) {
+    val_t local_energy_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t local_flux_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t local_energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
+    val_t local_energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
+
+#pragma omp parallel for reduction(+ : local_energy_sum, local_flux_sum) \
+    reduction(max : local_energy_max) reduction(min : local_energy_min) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < energy.size(); ++i) {
+        local_energy_sum += energy[i];
+        local_flux_sum += accumulated_flux[i];
+        local_energy_max = std::max(local_energy_max, energy[i]);
+        local_energy_min = std::min(local_energy_min, energy[i]);
+    }
+
+    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t energy_max = 0.0;
+    val_t energy_min = 0.0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_sum, &energy_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_flux_sum, &flux_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_max, &energy_max, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_min, &energy_min, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MIN, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+
+    int valid = 1;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        printf("Validation results:\n");
+        printf("  Energy sum: %.12f\n", energy_sum);
+        printf("  Flux sum: %.2f\n", flux_sum);
+        printf("  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", energy_min, energy_max);
+
+        constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {
+            printf("  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        } else if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {
+            printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {
+            printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
+            printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (valid != 0) {
+            printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    return valid != 0;
+}

using idx_t = uint64_t;
using val_t = double;

namespace {

constexpr int kThreadsPerBlock = 256;
constexpr int kBottomHaloTag = 100;
constexpr int kTopHaloTag = 101;

struct RowPartition {
    int first_row;
    int rows;
};

class CudaError final : public std::runtime_error {
public:
    explicit CudaError(const std::string& message) : std::runtime_error(message) {}
};

void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* expression, const char* file, int line) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        throw CudaError(std::string(file) + ":" + std::to_string(line) + ": " + expression +
                        " failed: " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
    }
}

#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) checkCuda((expression), #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__)

void checkMpi(int status, const char* expression) {
    if (status != MPI_SUCCESS) {
        char message[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING];
        int message_length = 0;
        MPI_Error_string(status, message, &message_length);
        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(expression) + " failed: " +
                                 std::string(message, message_length));
    }
}

#define MPI_CHECK(expression) checkMpi((expression), #expression)

RowPartition partitionRows(int grid_size, int rank, int ranks) {
    const int base_rows = grid_size / ranks;
    const int extra_rows = grid_size % ranks;
    const int rows = base_rows + (rank < extra_rows ? 1 : 0);
    const int first_row = rank * base_rows + std::min(rank, extra_rows);
    return {first_row, rows};
}

// The mesh is generated by buildSquare2D in the reference implementation.  Its
// connectivity is a regular four-neighbor grid, so storing that connectivity on
// every GPU is unnecessary.  This kernel evaluates exactly that generated mesh.
__global__ void updateRowsKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
                                 const val_t* __restrict__ accumulated_flux,
                                 val_t* __restrict__ next_energy,
                                 val_t* __restrict__ next_accumulated_flux,
                                 const val_t* __restrict__ top_halo,
                                 const val_t* __restrict__ bottom_halo,
                                 int grid_size,
                                 int first_global_row,
                                 int local_rows,
                                 int first_local_row,
                                 int end_local_row) {
    const size_t element_in_range = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    const size_t range_size = static_cast<size_t>(end_local_row - first_local_row) * grid_size;
    if (element_in_range >= range_size) {
        return;
    }

    const int local_row = first_local_row + static_cast<int>(element_in_range / grid_size);
    const int column = static_cast<int>(element_in_range % grid_size);
    const int global_row = first_global_row + local_row;
    const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(local_row) * grid_size + column;
    const val_t current = energy[element];

    // Materials are 0.8 transfer coefficient everywhere.  The four corner
    // assignments are kept in their original order (important for a 1x1 grid).
    val_t total_flux = 0.0;
    if ((global_row == 0 && column == 0) ||
        (global_row == grid_size - 1 && column == grid_size - 1)) {
        total_flux = 0.5;
    } else if ((global_row == 0 && column == grid_size - 1) ||
               (global_row == grid_size - 1 && column == 0)) {
        total_flux = -0.5;
    }

    // Preserve the reference connectivity traversal order: down, up, right, left.
    if (global_row + 1 < grid_size) {
        const val_t neighbor = local_row + 1 < local_rows
                                   ? energy[element + grid_size]
                                   : bottom_halo[column];
        total_flux += (neighbor - current) * 0.8 * 1.0 * 0.25;
    }
    if (global_row > 0) {
        const val_t neighbor = local_row > 0 ? energy[element - grid_size] : top_halo[column];
        total_flux += (neighbor - current) * 0.8 * 1.0 * 0.25;
    }
    if (column + 1 < grid_size) {
        total_flux += (energy[element + 1] - current) * 0.8 * 1.0 * 0.25;
    }
    if (column > 0) {
        total_flux += (energy[element - 1] - current) * 0.8 * 1.0 * 0.25;
    }

    next_energy[element] = current + total_flux;
    next_accumulated_flux[element] = accumulated_flux[element] + fabs(total_flux);
}

void launchRows(const val_t* energy,
                const val_t* accumulated_flux,
                val_t* next_energy,
                val_t* next_accumulated_flux,
                const val_t* top_halo,
                const val_t* bottom_halo,
                int grid_size,
                const RowPartition& partition,
                int first_local_row,
                int end_local_row,
                cudaStream_t stream) {
    if (first_local_row >= end_local_row) {
        return;
    }

    const size_t elements = static_cast<size_t>(end_local_row - first_local_row) * grid_size;
    const int blocks = static_cast<int>((elements + kThreadsPerBlock - 1) / kThreadsPerBlock);
    updateRowsKernel<<<blocks, kThreadsPerBlock, 0, stream>>>(
        energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux, top_halo, bottom_halo,
        grid_size, partition.first_row, partition.rows, first_local_row, end_local_row);
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
}

void runSimulation(const RowPartition& partition,
                   int grid_size,
                   int iterations,
                   int rank,
                   int ranks,
                   val_t*& energy,
                   val_t*& accumulated_flux,
                   val_t*& next_energy,
                   val_t*& next_accumulated_flux,
                   val_t* top_halo,
                   val_t* bottom_halo,
                   val_t* send_top,
                   val_t* send_bottom,
                   val_t* receive_top,
                   val_t* receive_bottom,
                   cudaStream_t compute_stream,
                   cudaStream_t boundary_stream,
                   cudaStream_t copy_stream) {
    const int previous_rank = rank == 0 ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank - 1;
    const int next_rank = rank + 1 == ranks ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank + 1;
    const size_t row_bytes = static_cast<size_t>(grid_size) * sizeof(val_t);

    for (int iteration = 0; iteration < iterations; ++iteration) {
        // Begin the host-staged halo exchange.  Pinned buffers make this work with
        // both CUDA-aware and conventional MPI implementations without a runtime
        // dependency on a particular MPI GPU transport.
        if (previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(send_top, energy, row_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, copy_stream));
        }
        if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(send_bottom,
                                       energy + static_cast<size_t>(partition.rows - 1) * grid_size,
                                       row_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, copy_stream));
        }

        // Interior rows do not depend on a halo and run while MPI transfers edges.
        launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
                   top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition, 1, partition.rows - 1,
                   compute_stream);

        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(copy_stream));

        MPI_Request requests[4];
        int request_count = 0;
        if (previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Irecv(receive_top, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, previous_rank, kBottomHaloTag,
                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Isend(send_top, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, previous_rank, kTopHaloTag,
                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
        }
        if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Irecv(receive_bottom, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, next_rank, kTopHaloTag,
                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Isend(send_bottom, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, next_rank, kBottomHaloTag,
                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
        }
        if (request_count != 0) {
            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Waitall(request_count, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE));
        }

        if (previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(top_halo, receive_top, row_bytes,
                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, copy_stream));
        }
        if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(bottom_halo, receive_bottom, row_bytes,
                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, copy_stream));
        }
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(copy_stream));

        // The boundary stream writes disjoint rows, so it overlaps the remaining
        // interior work.  A one-row partition has one boundary row, not two.
        if (partition.rows == 1) {
            launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
                       top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition, 0, 1, boundary_stream);
        } else {
            launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
                       top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition, 0, 1, boundary_stream);
            launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
                       top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition,
                       partition.rows - 1, partition.rows, boundary_stream);
        }

        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(compute_stream));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(boundary_stream));
        std::swap(energy, next_energy);
        std::swap(accumulated_flux, next_accumulated_flux);
    }
}

uint64_t computeLocalHash(const std::vector<val_t>& energy,
                          const std::vector<val_t>& accumulated_flux,
                          idx_t global_offset) {
    uint64_t local_hash = 0;
#pragma omp parallel for reduction(^ : local_hash) schedule(static)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < energy.size(); ++i) {
        const idx_t global_index = global_offset + i;
        const uint64_t energy_bits = std::bit_cast<uint64_t>(energy[i]);
        const uint64_t flux_bits = std::bit_cast<uint64_t>(accumulated_flux[i]);
        local_hash ^= (energy_bits + global_index) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
        local_hash ^= (flux_bits + global_index) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
    }
    return local_hash;
}

bool validateResults(const std::vector<val_t>& energy,
                     const std::vector<val_t>& accumulated_flux,
                     int rank) {
    val_t local_energy_sum = 0.0;
    val_t local_flux_sum = 0.0;
    val_t local_energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();

--- remaining source ---
    val_t local_energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();

#pragma omp parallel for reduction(+ : local_energy_sum, local_flux_sum) \
    reduction(max : local_energy_max) reduction(min : local_energy_min) schedule(static)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < energy.size(); ++i) {
        local_energy_sum += energy[i];
        local_flux_sum += accumulated_flux[i];
        local_energy_max = std::max(local_energy_max, energy[i]);
        local_energy_min = std::min(local_energy_min, energy[i]);
    }

    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;
    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;
    val_t energy_max = 0.0;
    val_t energy_min = 0.0;
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_sum, &energy_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_flux_sum, &flux_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_max, &energy_max, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_min, &energy_min, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MIN, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));

    int valid = 1;
    if (rank == 0) {
        printf("Validation results:\n");
        printf("  Energy sum: %.12f\n", energy_sum);
        printf("  Flux sum: %.2f\n", flux_sum);
        printf("  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", energy_min, energy_max);

        constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;
        if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {
            printf("  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\n");
            valid = 0;
        } else if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {
            printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");
        }
        if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {
            printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
            valid = 0;
        }
        if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
            printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
            valid = 0;
        }
        if (valid != 0) {
            printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
        }
    }
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
    return valid != 0;
}

void printUsage(const char* program_name) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", program_name);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

}  // namespace

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int mpi_initialized = 0;
    MPI_Initialized(&mpi_initialized);
    if (mpi_initialized == 0) {
        int provided_thread_level = MPI_THREAD_SINGLE;
        const int init_status = MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided_thread_level);
        if (init_status != MPI_SUCCESS) {
            fprintf(stderr, "MPI_Init_thread failed\n");
            return 1;
        }
        if (provided_thread_level < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) {
            fprintf(stderr, "MPI does not provide MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED support\n");
            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
            return 1;
        }
    }

    int rank = 0;
    int ranks = 1;
    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks);

    int exit_code = 0;
    val_t* energy_a = nullptr;
    val_t* energy_b = nullptr;
    val_t* flux_a = nullptr;
    val_t* flux_b = nullptr;
    val_t* top_halo = nullptr;
    val_t* bottom_halo = nullptr;
    val_t* send_top = nullptr;
    val_t* send_bottom = nullptr;
    val_t* receive_top = nullptr;
    val_t* receive_bottom = nullptr;
    cudaStream_t compute_stream = nullptr;
    cudaStream_t boundary_stream = nullptr;
    cudaStream_t copy_stream = nullptr;
    MPI_Comm local_comm = MPI_COMM_NULL;

    try {
        int grid_size = 512;
        int iterations = 10;
        bool validate = false;
        bool print_results_requested = false;

        for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
            if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
                grid_size = atoi(argv[++i]);
            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
                iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
                validate = true;
            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
                print_results_requested = true;
            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
                if (rank == 0) {
                    printUsage(argv[0]);
                }
                MPI_Finalize();
                return 0;
            } else {
                if (rank == 0) {
                    printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
                    printUsage(argv[0]);
                }
                MPI_Finalize();
                return 1;
            }
        }

        if (grid_size <= 0 || grid_size > std::numeric_limits<int>::max() / grid_size ||
            iterations < 0 || ranks > grid_size) {
            if (rank == 0) {
                fprintf(stderr, "Grid size must be positive, fit MPI counts, and have at least one row per MPI rank; iterations must be non-negative.\n");
            }
            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
            return 1;
        }

        const int global_elements = grid_size * grid_size;
        const RowPartition partition = partitionRows(grid_size, rank, ranks);
        const size_t local_elements = static_cast<size_t>(partition.rows) * grid_size;
        const size_t local_bytes = local_elements * sizeof(val_t);
        const size_t row_bytes = static_cast<size_t>(grid_size) * sizeof(val_t);

        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &local_comm));
        int local_rank = 0;
        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(local_comm, &local_rank));
        int device_count = 0;
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count));
        if (device_count == 0) {
            throw CudaError("No CUDA device is visible to this MPI rank");
        }
        const int device = local_rank % device_count;
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));  // Initialize the rank's CUDA context before timing.

        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&compute_stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&boundary_stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&copy_stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&energy_a, local_bytes));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&energy_b, local_bytes));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&flux_a, local_bytes));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&flux_b, local_bytes));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&top_halo, row_bytes));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&bottom_halo, row_bytes));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&send_top, row_bytes));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&send_bottom, row_bytes));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&receive_top, row_bytes));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&receive_bottom, row_bytes));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemsetAsync(energy_a, 0, local_bytes, compute_stream));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemsetAsync(flux_a, 0, local_bytes, compute_stream));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(compute_stream));

        if (rank == 0) {
            const size_t total_device_dynamic = static_cast<size_t>(global_elements) * 4 * sizeof(val_t);
            printf("Unstructured Mesh Energy Transfer Benchmark\n");
            printf("============================================\n");
            printf("Grid size: %d x %d = %d elements\n", grid_size, grid_size, global_elements);
            printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
            printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
            printf("Parallel execution: %d MPI ranks, CUDA device per local rank, %d OpenMP host threads/rank\n",
                   ranks, omp_get_max_threads());
            printf("Distributed device state: %.2f MB aggregate (four double-precision fields)\n\n",
                   total_device_dynamic / (1024.0 * 1024.0));
            printf("Running simulation...\n");
        }
        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));

        const auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
        runSimulation(partition, grid_size, iterations, rank, ranks,
                      energy_a, flux_a, energy_b, flux_b,
                      top_halo, bottom_halo, send_top, send_bottom, receive_top, receive_bottom,
                      compute_stream, boundary_stream, copy_stream);
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));
        const auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

        std::vector<val_t> host_energy(local_elements);
        std::vector<val_t> host_flux(local_elements);
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host_energy.data(), energy_a, local_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host_flux.data(), flux_a, local_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));

        const uint64_t local_hash = computeLocalHash(
            host_energy, host_flux, static_cast<idx_t>(partition.first_row) * grid_size);
        uint64_t global_hash = 0;
        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_hash, &global_hash, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_BXOR, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));

        if (rank == 0) {
            const double elapsed_seconds = std::chrono::duration<double>(end - start).count();
            const double elapsed_ms = elapsed_seconds * 1000.0;
            const int measured_iterations = std::max(iterations - 1, 1);
            const double time_per_iteration = elapsed_ms / measured_iterations;
            const double giga_elements_per_second = elapsed_seconds > 0.0
                ? static_cast<double>(measured_iterations) * global_elements / elapsed_seconds / 1.0e9
                : 0.0;
            printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", elapsed_ms);
            printf("Performance:\n");
            printf("  Time per iteration: %.4f ms\n", time_per_iteration);
            printf("  Elements/sec: %.4f GigaElements/s\n", giga_elements_per_second);
            printf("  Performance: %.4f GFLOPS\n", giga_elements_per_second * 22.0);
            printf("  Result hash: %016llX\n\n", static_cast<unsigned long long>(global_hash));
        }

        if (print_results_requested) {
            std::vector<int> receive_counts;
            std::vector<int> displacements;
            std::vector<val_t> global_energy;
            if (rank == 0) {
                receive_counts.resize(ranks);
                displacements.resize(ranks);
                for (int process = 0; process < ranks; ++process) {
                    const RowPartition process_partition = partitionRows(grid_size, process, ranks);
                    receive_counts[process] = process_partition.rows * grid_size;
                    displacements[process] = process_partition.first_row * grid_size;
                }
                global_energy.resize(global_elements);
            }
            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Gatherv(host_energy.data(), static_cast<int>(local_elements), MPI_DOUBLE,
                                  rank == 0 ? global_energy.data() : nullptr,
                                  rank == 0 ? receive_counts.data() : nullptr,
                                  rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
                                  MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
            if (rank == 0) {
                print_results(global_energy, "ElementEnergy");
            }
        }

        if (validate && !validateResults(host_energy, host_flux, rank)) {
            exit_code = 1;
        }
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, error.what());
        exit_code = 1;
    }

    if (copy_stream != nullptr) cudaStreamDestroy(copy_stream);
    if (boundary_stream != nullptr) cudaStreamDestroy(boundary_stream);
    if (compute_stream != nullptr) cudaStreamDestroy(compute_stream);
    if (receive_bottom != nullptr) cudaFreeHost(receive_bottom);
    if (receive_top != nullptr) cudaFreeHost(receive_top);
    if (send_bottom != nullptr) cudaFreeHost(send_bottom);
    if (send_top != nullptr) cudaFreeHost(send_top);
    if (bottom_halo != nullptr) cudaFree(bottom_halo);
    if (top_halo != nullptr) cudaFree(top_halo);
    if (flux_b != nullptr) cudaFree(flux_b);
    if (flux_a != nullptr) cudaFree(flux_a);
    if (energy_b != nullptr) cudaFree(energy_b);
    if (energy_a != nullptr) cudaFree(energy_a);
    if (local_comm != MPI_COMM_NULL) MPI_Comm_free(&local_comm);

    int global_exit_code = 0;
    MPI_Allreduce(&exit_code, &global_exit_code, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    MPI_Finalize();
    return global_exit_code;
}

--- CMake ---
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.24)
project(unstructured_benchmark LANGUAGES CXX CUDA)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

# Build native SASS by default for the accelerator on which the benchmark is
# configured.  Sites building for a different cluster can override this cache
# entry with their required CUDA architecture list.
if(NOT DEFINED CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES)
    set(CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native CACHE STRING "CUDA architectures to build for")
endif()

find_package(MPI REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)
find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)
find_package(CUDAToolkit REQUIRED)

add_executable(unstructured unstructured.cpp)
set_source_files_properties(unstructured.cpp PROPERTIES LANGUAGE CUDA)

set_target_properties(unstructured PROPERTIES
    CUDA_SEPARABLE_COMPILATION OFF
    CUDA_RESOLVE_DEVICE_SYMBOLS OFF
)

# CUDA owns this translation unit, so forward CPU optimization and OpenMP flags
# explicitly to its host compiler as well as optimizing device code.
target_compile_options(unstructured PRIVATE
    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-O3;-lineinfo;-Xcompiler=-O3,-march=native,-Wall,-Wextra,-fopenmp>
)

target_link_libraries(unstructured PRIVATE
    MPI::MPI_CXX
    OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX
    CUDA::cudart
    m
)

-// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification
-uint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {
-    uint64_t hash = 0;
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {

-        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);

+void runSimulation(const RowPartition& partition,
+                   int grid_size,
+                   int iterations,
+                   int rank,
+                   int ranks,
+                   val_t*& energy,
+                   val_t*& accumulated_flux,
+                   val_t*& next_energy,
+                   val_t*& next_accumulated_flux,
+                   val_t* top_halo,
+                   val_t* bottom_halo,
+                   val_t* send_top,
+                   val_t* send_bottom,
+                   val_t* receive_top,
+                   val_t* receive_bottom,
+                   cudaStream_t compute_stream,
+                   cudaStream_t boundary_stream,
+                   cudaStream_t copy_stream) {
+    const int previous_rank = rank == 0 ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank - 1;
+    const int next_rank = rank + 1 == ranks ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank + 1;
+    const size_t row_bytes = static_cast<size_t>(grid_size) * sizeof(val_t);
+
+    for (int iteration = 0; iteration < iterations; ++iteration) {
+        // Begin the host-staged halo exchange.  Pinned buffers make this work with
+        // both CUDA-aware and conventional MPI implementations without a runtime
+        // dependency on a particular MPI GPU transport.
+        if (previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(send_top, energy, row_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, copy_stream));
+        }
+        if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(send_bottom,
+                                       energy + static_cast<size_t>(partition.rows - 1) * grid_size,
+                                       row_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, copy_stream));
+        }
+
+        // Interior rows do not depend on a halo and run while MPI transfers edges.
+        launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
+                   top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition, 1, partition.rows - 1,
+                   compute_stream);
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(copy_stream));
+
+        MPI_Request requests[4];
+        int request_count = 0;
+        if (previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Irecv(receive_top, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, previous_rank, kBottomHaloTag,
+                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Isend(send_top, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, previous_rank, kTopHaloTag,
+                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
+        }
+        if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Irecv(receive_bottom, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, next_rank, kTopHaloTag,
+                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Isend(send_bottom, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, next_rank, kBottomHaloTag,
+                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
+        }
+        if (request_count != 0) {
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Waitall(request_count, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE));
+        }
+
+        if (previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(top_halo, receive_top, row_bytes,
+                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, copy_stream));
+        }
+        if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(bottom_halo, receive_bottom, row_bytes,
+                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, copy_stream));
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(copy_stream));
+
+        // The boundary stream writes disjoint rows, so it overlaps the remaining
+        // interior work.  A one-row partition has one boundary row, not two.
+        if (partition.rows == 1) {
+            launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
+                       top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition, 0, 1, boundary_stream);
+        } else {
+            launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
+                       top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition, 0, 1, boundary_stream);
+            launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
+                       top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition,
+                       partition.rows - 1, partition.rows, boundary_stream);
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(compute_stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(boundary_stream));
+        std::swap(energy, next_energy);
+        std::swap(accumulated_flux, next_accumulated_flux);
     }
-    return hash;
 }

+uint64_t computeLocalHash(const std::vector<val_t>& energy,
+                          const std::vector<val_t>& accumulated_flux,
+                          idx_t global_offset) {
+    uint64_t local_hash = 0;
+#pragma omp parallel for reduction(^ : local_hash) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < energy.size(); ++i) {
+        const idx_t global_index = global_offset + i;
+        const uint64_t energy_bits = std::bit_cast<uint64_t>(energy[i]);
+        const uint64_t flux_bits = std::bit_cast<uint64_t>(accumulated_flux[i]);
+        local_hash ^= (energy_bits + global_index) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        local_hash ^= (flux_bits + global_index) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+    }
+    return local_hash;
+}
+
+bool validateResults(const std::vector<val_t>& energy,
+                     const std::vector<val_t>& accumulated_flux,
+                     int rank) {
+    val_t local_energy_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t local_flux_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t local_energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
+    val_t local_energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
+
+#pragma omp parallel for reduction(+ : local_energy_sum, local_flux_sum) \
+    reduction(max : local_energy_max) reduction(min : local_energy_min) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < energy.size(); ++i) {
+        local_energy_sum += energy[i];
+        local_flux_sum += accumulated_flux[i];
+        local_energy_max = std::max(local_energy_max, energy[i]);
+        local_energy_min = std::min(local_energy_min, energy[i]);
+    }
+
+    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t energy_max = 0.0;
+    val_t energy_min = 0.0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_sum, &energy_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_flux_sum, &flux_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_max, &energy_max, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_min, &energy_min, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MIN, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+
+    int valid = 1;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        printf("Validation results:\n");
+        printf("  Energy sum: %.12f\n", energy_sum);
+        printf("  Flux sum: %.2f\n", flux_sum);
+        printf("  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", energy_min, energy_max);
+
+        constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {
+            printf("  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        } else if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {
+            printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {
+            printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
+            printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (valid != 0) {
+            printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    return valid != 0;
+}

+    int exit_code = 0;
+    val_t* energy_a = nullptr;
+    val_t* energy_b = nullptr;
+    val_t* flux_a = nullptr;
+    val_t* flux_b = nullptr;
+    val_t* top_halo = nullptr;
+    val_t* bottom_halo = nullptr;
+    val_t* send_top = nullptr;
+    val_t* send_bottom = nullptr;
+    val_t* receive_top = nullptr;
+    val_t* receive_bottom = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t compute_stream = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t boundary_stream = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t copy_stream = nullptr;
+    MPI_Comm local_comm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+
+    try {
+        int grid_size = 512;
+        int iterations = 10;
+        bool validate = false;
+        bool print_results_requested = false;
+
+        for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
+            if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
+                grid_size = atoi(argv[++i]);
+            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
+                iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
+            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
+                validate = true;
+            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
+                print_results_requested = true;
+            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
+                if (rank == 0) {
+                    printUsage(argv[0]);
+                }
+                MPI_Finalize();
+                return 0;
+            } else {
+                if (rank == 0) {
+                    printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
+                    printUsage(argv[0]);
+                }
+                MPI_Finalize();
+                return 1;
+            }
         }
-        print_results(energyData, "ElementEnergy");
-    }
-    
-    // Validation
-    if (validate) {
-        bool valid = validateResults(world);
-        if (!valid) {
+
+        if (grid_size <= 0 || grid_size > std::numeric_limits<int>::max() / grid_size ||
+            iterations < 0 || ranks > grid_size) {
+            if (rank == 0) {
+                fprintf(stderr, "Grid size must be positive, fit MPI counts, and have at least one row per MPI rank; iterations must be non-negative.\n");
+            }
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
             return 1;
         }
+
+        const int global_elements = grid_size * grid_size;
+        const RowPartition partition = partitionRows(grid_size, rank, ranks);
+        const size_t local_elements = static_cast<size_t>(partition.rows) * grid_size;
+        const size_t local_bytes = local_elements * sizeof(val_t);
+        const size_t row_bytes = static_cast<size_t>(grid_size) * sizeof(val_t);
+
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &local_comm));
+        int local_rank = 0;
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(local_comm, &local_rank));
+        int device_count = 0;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count));
+        if (device_count == 0) {
+            throw CudaError("No CUDA device is visible to this MPI rank");
+        }
+        const int device = local_rank % device_count;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));  // Initialize the rank's CUDA context before timing.
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&compute_stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&boundary_stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&copy_stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&energy_a, local_bytes));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&energy_b, local_bytes));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&flux_a, local_bytes));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&flux_b, local_bytes));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&top_halo, row_bytes));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&bottom_halo, row_bytes));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&send_top, row_bytes));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&send_bottom, row_bytes));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&receive_top, row_bytes));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&receive_bottom, row_bytes));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemsetAsync(energy_a, 0, local_bytes, compute_stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemsetAsync(flux_a, 0, local_bytes, compute_stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(compute_stream));
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            const size_t total_device_dynamic = static_cast<size_t>(global_elements) * 4 * sizeof(val_t);
+            printf("Unstructured Mesh Energy Transfer Benchmark\n");
+            printf("============================================\n");
+            printf("Grid size: %d x %d = %d elements\n", grid_size, grid_size, global_elements);
+            printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
+            printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
+            printf("Parallel execution: %d MPI ranks, CUDA device per local rank, %d OpenMP host threads/rank\n",
+                   ranks, omp_get_max_threads());
+            printf("Distributed device state: %.2f MB aggregate (four double-precision fields)\n\n",
+                   total_device_dynamic / (1024.0 * 1024.0));
+            printf("Running simulation...\n");
+        }
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+
+        const auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
+        runSimulation(partition, grid_size, iterations, rank, ranks,
+                      energy_a, flux_a, energy_b, flux_b,
+                      top_halo, bottom_halo, send_top, send_bottom, receive_top, receive_bottom,
+                      compute_stream, boundary_stream, copy_stream);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+        const auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
+
+        std::vector<val_t> host_energy(local_elements);
+        std::vector<val_t> host_flux(local_elements);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host_energy.data(), energy_a, local_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host_flux.data(), flux_a, local_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+
+        const uint64_t local_hash = computeLocalHash(
+            host_energy, host_flux, static_cast<idx_t>(partition.first_row) * grid_size);
+        uint64_t global_hash = 0;
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_hash, &global_hash, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_BXOR, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            const double elapsed_seconds = std::chrono::duration<double>(end - start).count();
+            const double elapsed_ms = elapsed_seconds * 1000.0;
+            const int measured_iterations = std::max(iterations - 1, 1);
+            const double time_per_iteration = elapsed_ms / measured_iterations;
+            const double giga_elements_per_second = elapsed_seconds > 0.0
+                ? static_cast<double>(measured_iterations) * global_elements / elapsed_seconds / 1.0e9
+                : 0.0;
+            printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", elapsed_ms);
+            printf("Performance:\n");
+            printf("  Time per iteration: %.4f ms\n", time_per_iteration);
+            printf("  Elements/sec: %.4f GigaElements/s\n", giga_elements_per_second);
+            printf("  Performance: %.4f GFLOPS\n", giga_elements_per_second * 22.0);
+            printf("  Result hash: %016llX\n\n", static_cast<unsigned long long>(global_hash));
+        }
+
+        if (print_results_requested) {
+            std::vector<int> receive_counts;
+            std::vector<int> displacements;
+            std::vector<val_t> global_energy;
+            if (rank == 0) {
+                receive_counts.resize(ranks);
+                displacements.resize(ranks);
+                for (int process = 0; process < ranks; ++process) {
+                    const RowPartition process_partition = partitionRows(grid_size, process, ranks);
+                    receive_counts[process] = process_partition.rows * grid_size;
+                    displacements[process] = process_partition.first_row * grid_size;
+                }
+                global_energy.resize(global_elements);
+            }
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Gatherv(host_energy.data(), static_cast<int>(local_elements), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                                  rank == 0 ? global_energy.data() : nullptr,
+                                  rank == 0 ? receive_counts.data() : nullptr,
+                                  rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                                  MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+            if (rank == 0) {
+                print_results(global_energy, "ElementEnergy");
+            }
+        }
+
+        if (validate && !validateResults(host_energy, host_flux, rank)) {
+            exit_code = 1;
+        }
+    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, error.what());
+        exit_code = 1;
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    if (copy_stream != nullptr) cudaStreamDestroy(copy_stream);
+    if (boundary_stream != nullptr) cudaStreamDestroy(boundary_stream);
+    if (compute_stream != nullptr) cudaStreamDestroy(compute_stream);
+    if (receive_bottom != nullptr) cudaFreeHost(receive_bottom);
+    if (receive_top != nullptr) cudaFreeHost(receive_top);
+    if (send_bottom != nullptr) cudaFreeHost(send_bottom);
+    if (send_top != nullptr) cudaFreeHost(send_top);
+    if (bottom_halo != nullptr) cudaFree(bottom_halo);
+    if (top_halo != nullptr) cudaFree(top_halo);
+    if (flux_b != nullptr) cudaFree(flux_b);
+    if (flux_a != nullptr) cudaFree(flux_a);
+    if (energy_b != nullptr) cudaFree(energy_b);
+    if (energy_a != nullptr) cudaFree(energy_a);
+    if (local_comm != MPI_COMM_NULL) MPI_Comm_free(&local_comm);
+
+    int global_exit_code = 0;
+    MPI_Allreduce(&exit_code, &global_exit_code, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return global_exit_code;
 }

+void launchRows(const val_t* energy,
+                const val_t* accumulated_flux,
+                val_t* next_energy,
+                val_t* next_accumulated_flux,
+                const val_t* top_halo,
+                const val_t* bottom_halo,

+

+    const int blocks = static_cast<int>((elements + kThreadsPerBlock - 1) / kThreadsPerBlock);
+    updateRowsKernel<<<blocks, kThreadsPerBlock, 0, stream>>>(
+        energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux, top_halo, bottom_halo,
+        grid_size, partition.first_row, partition.rows, first_local_row, end_local_row);

-uint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {
-    uint64_t hash = 0;
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {

+void runSimulation(const RowPartition& partition,
+                   int grid_size,
+                   int iterations,
+                   int rank,
+                   int ranks,
+                   val_t*& energy,
+                   val_t*& accumulated_flux,
+                   val_t*& next_energy,
+                   val_t*& next_accumulated_flux,
+                   val_t* top_halo,
+                   val_t* bottom_halo,
+                   val_t* send_top,
+                   val_t* send_bottom,
+                   val_t* receive_top,
+                   val_t* receive_bottom,
+                   cudaStream_t compute_stream,
+                   cudaStream_t boundary_stream,
+                   cudaStream_t copy_stream) {
+    const int previous_rank = rank == 0 ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank - 1;
+    const int next_rank = rank + 1 == ranks ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank + 1;
+    const size_t row_bytes = static_cast<size_t>(grid_size) * sizeof(val_t);
+
+    for (int iteration = 0; iteration < iterations; ++iteration) {
+        // Begin the host-staged halo exchange.  Pinned buffers make this work with
+        // both CUDA-aware and conventional MPI implementations without a runtime
+        // dependency on a particular MPI GPU transport.
+        if (previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(send_top, energy, row_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, copy_stream));
+        }
+        if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(send_bottom,
+                                       energy + static_cast<size_t>(partition.rows - 1) * grid_size,
+                                       row_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, copy_stream));
+        }
+
+        // Interior rows do not depend on a halo and run while MPI transfers edges.
+        launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
+                   top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition, 1, partition.rows - 1,
+                   compute_stream);
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(copy_stream));
+
+        MPI_Request requests[4];
+        int request_count = 0;
+        if (previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Irecv(receive_top, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, previous_rank, kBottomHaloTag,
+                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Isend(send_top, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, previous_rank, kTopHaloTag,
+                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
+        }
+        if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Irecv(receive_bottom, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, next_rank, kTopHaloTag,
+                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Isend(send_bottom, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, next_rank, kBottomHaloTag,
+                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
+        }
+        if (request_count != 0) {
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Waitall(request_count, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE));
+        }
+
+        if (previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(top_halo, receive_top, row_bytes,
+                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, copy_stream));
+        }
+        if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(bottom_halo, receive_bottom, row_bytes,
+                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, copy_stream));
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(copy_stream));
+
+        // The boundary stream writes disjoint rows, so it overlaps the remaining
+        // interior work.  A one-row partition has one boundary row, not two.
+        if (partition.rows == 1) {
+            launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
+                       top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition, 0, 1, boundary_stream);
+        } else {
+            launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
+                       top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition, 0, 1, boundary_stream);
+            launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
+                       top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition,
+                       partition.rows - 1, partition.rows, boundary_stream);
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(compute_stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(boundary_stream));
+        std::swap(energy, next_energy);
+        std::swap(accumulated_flux, next_accumulated_flux);
     }
-    return hash;
 }
 
-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
+uint64_t computeLocalHash(const std::vector<val_t>& energy,
+                          const std::vector<val_t>& accumulated_flux,
+                          idx_t global_offset) {
+    uint64_t local_hash = 0;
+#pragma omp parallel for reduction(^ : local_hash) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < energy.size(); ++i) {
+        const idx_t global_index = global_offset + i;
+        const uint64_t energy_bits = std::bit_cast<uint64_t>(energy[i]);
+        const uint64_t flux_bits = std::bit_cast<uint64_t>(accumulated_flux[i]);
+        local_hash ^= (energy_bits + global_index) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        local_hash ^= (flux_bits + global_index) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+    }
+    return local_hash;
+}
+
+bool validateResults(const std::vector<val_t>& energy,
+                     const std::vector<val_t>& accumulated_flux,
+                     int rank) {
+    val_t local_energy_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t local_flux_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t local_energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
+    val_t local_energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
+
+#pragma omp parallel for reduction(+ : local_energy_sum, local_flux_sum) \
+    reduction(max : local_energy_max) reduction(min : local_energy_min) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < energy.size(); ++i) {
+        local_energy_sum += energy[i];
+        local_flux_sum += accumulated_flux[i];
+        local_energy_max = std::max(local_energy_max, energy[i]);
+        local_energy_min = std::min(local_energy_min, energy[i]);
+    }
+
+    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t energy_max = 0.0;
+    val_t energy_min = 0.0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_sum, &energy_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_flux_sum, &flux_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_max, &energy_max, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_min, &energy_min, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MIN, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+
+    int valid = 1;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        printf("Validation results:\n");
+        printf("  Energy sum: %.12f\n", energy_sum);
+        printf("  Flux sum: %.2f\n", flux_sum);
+        printf("  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", energy_min, energy_max);
+
+        constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {
+            printf("  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        } else if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {
+            printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {
+            printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
+            printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (valid != 0) {
+            printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    return valid != 0;
+}
+

+            printf("  Performance: %.4f GFLOPS\n", giga_elements_per_second * 22.0);
+            printf("  Result hash: %016llX\n\n", static_cast<unsigned long long>(global_hash));
+        }

+        if (print_results_requested) {
+            std::vector<int> receive_counts;
+            std::vector<int> displacements;

+            if (rank == 0) {
+                receive_counts.resize(ranks);
+                displacements.resize(ranks);
+                for (int process = 0; process < ranks; ++process) {
+                    const RowPartition process_partition = partitionRows(grid_size, process, ranks);
+                    receive_counts[process] = process_partition.rows * grid_size;
+                    displacements[process] = process_partition.first_row * grid_size;
+                }
+                global_energy.resize(global_elements);

+                                  rank == 0 ? receive_counts.data() : nullptr,
+                                  rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                                  MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));

+            }
+        }
+
+        if (validate && !validateResults(host_energy, host_flux, rank)) {
+            exit_code = 1;
+        }

+        exit_code = 1;

+void launchRows(const val_t* energy,
+                const val_t* accumulated_flux,
+                val_t* next_energy,
+                val_t* next_accumulated_flux,

-// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification
-uint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {
-    uint64_t hash = 0;
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {
