    60	RowPartition partitionRows(int grid_size, int rank, int ranks) {
    61	    const int base_rows = grid_size / ranks;
    62	    const int extra_rows = grid_size % ranks;
    63	    const int rows = base_rows + (rank < extra_rows ? 1 : 0);
    64	    const int first_row = rank * base_rows + std::min(rank, extra_rows);
    65	    return {first_row, rows};
    66	}
    67	
    68	// The mesh is generated by buildSquare2D in the reference implementation.  Its
    69	// connectivity is a regular four-neighbor grid, so storing that connectivity on
    70	// every GPU is unnecessary.  This kernel evaluates exactly that generated mesh.
    71	__global__ void updateRowsKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ energy,
    72	                                 const val_t* __restrict__ accumulated_flux,
    73	                                 val_t* __restrict__ next_energy,
    74	                                 val_t* __restrict__ next_accumulated_flux,
    75	                                 const val_t* __restrict__ top_halo,
    76	                                 const val_t* __restrict__ bottom_halo,
    77	                                 int grid_size,
    78	                                 int first_global_row,
    79	                                 int local_rows,
    80	                                 int first_local_row,
    81	                                 int end_local_row) {
    82	    const size_t element_in_range = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    83	    const size_t range_size = static_cast<size_t>(end_local_row - first_local_row) * grid_size;
    84	    if (element_in_range >= range_size) {
    85	        return;
    86	    }
    87	
    88	    const int local_row = first_local_row + static_cast<int>(element_in_range / grid_size);
    89	    const int column = static_cast<int>(element_in_range % grid_size);
    90	    const int global_row = first_global_row + local_row;
    91	    const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(local_row) * grid_size + column;
    92	    const val_t current = energy[element];
    93	
    94	    // Materials are 0.8 transfer coefficient everywhere.  The four corner
    95	    // assignments are kept in their original order (important for a 1x1 grid).
    96	    val_t total_flux = 0.0;
    97	    if ((global_row == 0 && column == 0) ||
    98	        (global_row == grid_size - 1 && column == grid_size - 1)) {
    99	        total_flux = 0.5;
   100	    } else if ((global_row == 0 && column == grid_size - 1) ||
   101	               (global_row == grid_size - 1 && column == 0)) {
   102	        total_flux = -0.5;
   103	    }
   104	
   105	    // Preserve the reference connectivity traversal order: down, up, right, left.
   106	    if (global_row + 1 < grid_size) {
   107	        const val_t neighbor = local_row + 1 < local_rows
   108	                                   ? energy[element + grid_size]
   109	                                   : bottom_halo[column];
   110	        total_flux += (neighbor - current) * 0.8 * 1.0 * 0.25;
   111	    }
   112	    if (global_row > 0) {
   113	        const val_t neighbor = local_row > 0 ? energy[element - grid_size] : top_halo[column];
   114	        total_flux += (neighbor - current) * 0.8 * 1.0 * 0.25;
   115	    }
   116	    if (column + 1 < grid_size) {
   117	        total_flux += (energy[element + 1] - current) * 0.8 * 1.0 * 0.25;
   118	    }
   119	    if (column > 0) {
   120	        total_flux += (energy[element - 1] - current) * 0.8 * 1.0 * 0.25;
   121	    }
   122	
   123	    next_energy[element] = current + total_flux;
   124	    next_accumulated_flux[element] = accumulated_flux[element] + fabs(total_flux);
   125	}
   126	
   127	void launchRows(const val_t* energy,
   128	                const val_t* accumulated_flux,
   129	                val_t* next_energy,
   130	                val_t* next_accumulated_flux,
   131	                const val_t* top_halo,
   132	                const val_t* bottom_halo,
   133	                int grid_size,
   134	                const RowPartition& partition,
   135	                int first_local_row,
   136	                int end_local_row,
   137	                cudaStream_t stream) {
   138	    if (first_local_row >= end_local_row) {
   139	        return;
   140	    }
   141	
   142	    const size_t elements = static_cast<size_t>(end_local_row - first_local_row) * grid_size;
   143	    const int blocks = static_cast<int>((elements + kThreadsPerBlock - 1) / kThreadsPerBlock);
   144	    updateRowsKernel<<<blocks, kThreadsPerBlock, 0, stream>>>(
   145	        energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux, top_halo, bottom_halo,
   146	        grid_size, partition.first_row, partition.rows, first_local_row, end_local_row);
   147	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   148	}
   149	
   150	void runSimulation(const RowPartition& partition,
   151	                   int grid_size,
   152	                   int iterations,
   153	                   int rank,
   154	                   int ranks,
   155	                   val_t*& energy,
   156	                   val_t*& accumulated_flux,
   157	                   val_t*& next_energy,
   158	                   val_t*& next_accumulated_flux,
   159	                   val_t* top_halo,
   160	                   val_t* bottom_halo,
   161	                   val_t* send_top,
   162	                   val_t* send_bottom,
   163	                   val_t* receive_top,
   164	                   val_t* receive_bottom,
   165	                   cudaStream_t compute_stream,
   166	                   cudaStream_t boundary_stream,
   167	                   cudaStream_t copy_stream) {
   168	    const int previous_rank = rank == 0 ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank - 1;
   169	    const int next_rank = rank + 1 == ranks ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank + 1;
   170	    const size_t row_bytes = static_cast<size_t>(grid_size) * sizeof(val_t);
   171	
   172	    for (int iteration = 0; iteration < iterations; ++iteration) {
   173	        // Begin the host-staged halo exchange.  Pinned buffers make this work with
   174	        // both CUDA-aware and conventional MPI implementations without a runtime
   175	        // dependency on a particular MPI GPU transport.
   176	        if (previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
   177	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(send_top, energy, row_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, copy_stream));
   178	        }
   179	        if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
   180	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(send_bottom,
   181	                                       energy + static_cast<size_t>(partition.rows - 1) * grid_size,
   182	                                       row_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, copy_stream));
   183	        }
   184	
   185	        // Interior rows do not depend on a halo and run while MPI transfers edges.
   186	        launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
   187	                   top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition, 1, partition.rows - 1,
   188	                   compute_stream);
   189	
   190	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(copy_stream));
   191	
   192	        MPI_Request requests[4];
   193	        int request_count = 0;
   194	        if (previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
   195	            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Irecv(receive_top, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, previous_rank, kBottomHaloTag,
   196	                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
   197	            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Isend(send_top, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, previous_rank, kTopHaloTag,
   198	                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
   199	        }
   200	        if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
   201	            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Irecv(receive_bottom, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, next_rank, kTopHaloTag,
   202	                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
   203	            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Isend(send_bottom, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, next_rank, kBottomHaloTag,
   204	                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
   205	        }
   206	        if (request_count != 0) {
   207	            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Waitall(request_count, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE));
   208	        }
   209	
   210	        if (previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
   211	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(top_halo, receive_top, row_bytes,
   212	                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, copy_stream));
   213	        }
   214	        if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
   215	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(bottom_halo, receive_bottom, row_bytes,
   216	                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, copy_stream));
   217	        }
   218	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(copy_stream));
   219	
   220	        // The boundary stream writes disjoint rows, so it overlaps the remaining
   221	        // interior work.  A one-row partition has one boundary row, not two.
   222	        if (partition.rows == 1) {
   223	            launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
   224	                       top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition, 0, 1, boundary_stream);
   225	        } else {
   226	            launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
   227	                       top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition, 0, 1, boundary_stream);
   228	            launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
   229	                       top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition,
   230	                       partition.rows - 1, partition.rows, boundary_stream);
   231	        }
   232	
   233	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(compute_stream));
   234	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(boundary_stream));
   235	        std::swap(energy, next_energy);
   236	        std::swap(accumulated_flux, next_accumulated_flux);
   237	    }
   238	}

   240	uint64_t computeLocalHash(const std::vector<val_t>& energy,

   367	        for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   368	            if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   369	                grid_size = atoi(argv[++i]);
   370	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   371	                iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
   372	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   373	                validate = true;
   374	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   375	                print_results_requested = true;
   376	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   377	                if (rank == 0) {
   378	                    printUsage(argv[0]);
   379	                }
   380	                MPI_Finalize();
   381	                return 0;
   382	            } else {
   383	                if (rank == 0) {
   384	                    printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   385	                    printUsage(argv[0]);
   386	                }
   387	                MPI_Finalize();
   388	                return 1;
   389	            }
   390	        }
   391	
   392	        if (grid_size <= 0 || grid_size > std::numeric_limits<int>::max() / grid_size ||
   393	            iterations < 0 || ranks > grid_size) {
   394	            if (rank == 0) {
   395	                fprintf(stderr, "Grid size must be positive, fit MPI counts, and have at least one row per MPI rank; iterations must be non-negative.\n");
   396	            }
   397	            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
   398	            return 1;
   399	        }
   400	
   401	        const int global_elements = grid_size * grid_size;
   402	        const RowPartition partition = partitionRows(grid_size, rank, ranks);
   403	        const size_t local_elements = static_cast<size_t>(partition.rows) * grid_size;
   404	        const size_t local_bytes = local_elements * sizeof(val_t);
   405	        const size_t row_bytes = static_cast<size_t>(grid_size) * sizeof(val_t);
   406	
   407	        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &local_comm));
   408	        int local_rank = 0;
   409	        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(local_comm, &local_rank));
   410	        int device_count = 0;
   411	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count));
   412	        if (device_count == 0) {
   413	            throw CudaError("No CUDA device is visible to this MPI rank");
   414	        }
   415	        const int device = local_rank % device_count;
   416	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));
   417	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));  // Initialize the rank's CUDA context before timing.
   418	
   419	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&compute_stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
   420	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&boundary_stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
   421	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&copy_stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
   422	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&energy_a, local_bytes));
   423	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&energy_b, local_bytes));
   424	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&flux_a, local_bytes));
   425	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&flux_b, local_bytes));
   426	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&top_halo, row_bytes));
   427	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&bottom_halo, row_bytes));
   428	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&send_top, row_bytes));
   429	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&send_bottom, row_bytes));
   430	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&receive_top, row_bytes));
   431	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&receive_bottom, row_bytes));
   432	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemsetAsync(energy_a, 0, local_bytes, compute_stream));
   433	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemsetAsync(flux_a, 0, local_bytes, compute_stream));
   434	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(compute_stream));
   435	
   436	        if (rank == 0) {
   437	            const size_t total_device_dynamic = static_cast<size_t>(global_elements) * 4 * sizeof(val_t);
   438	            printf("Unstructured Mesh Energy Transfer Benchmark\n");
   439	            printf("============================================\n");
   440	            printf("Grid size: %d x %d = %d elements\n", grid_size, grid_size, global_elements);
   441	            printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
   442	            printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   443	            printf("Parallel execution: %d MPI ranks, CUDA device per local rank, %d OpenMP host threads/rank\n",
   444	                   ranks, omp_get_max_threads());
   445	            printf("Distributed device state: %.2f MB aggregate (four double-precision fields)\n\n",
   446	                   total_device_dynamic / (1024.0 * 1024.0));
   447	            printf("Running simulation...\n");
   448	        }
   449	        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   450	
   451	        const auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   452	        runSimulation(partition, grid_size, iterations, rank, ranks,
   453	                      energy_a, flux_a, energy_b, flux_b,
   454	                      top_halo, bottom_halo, send_top, send_bottom, receive_top, receive_bottom,
   455	                      compute_stream, boundary_stream, copy_stream);
   456	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
   457	        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   458	        const auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   459	
   460	        std::vector<val_t> host_energy(local_elements);
   461	        std::vector<val_t> host_flux(local_elements);
   462	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host_energy.data(), energy_a, local_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   463	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host_flux.data(), flux_a, local_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   464	
   465	        const uint64_t local_hash = computeLocalHash(
   466	            host_energy, host_flux, static_cast<idx_t>(partition.first_row) * grid_size);
   467	        uint64_t global_hash = 0;
   468	        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_hash, &global_hash, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_BXOR, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   469	
   470	        if (rank == 0) {
   471	            const double elapsed_seconds = std::chrono::duration<double>(end - start).count();
   472	            const double elapsed_ms = elapsed_seconds * 1000.0;
   473	            const int measured_iterations = std::max(iterations - 1, 1);
   474	            const double time_per_iteration = elapsed_ms / measured_iterations;
   475	            const double giga_elements_per_second = elapsed_seconds > 0.0
   476	                ? static_cast<double>(measured_iterations) * global_elements / elapsed_seconds / 1.0e9
   477	                : 0.0;
   478	            printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", elapsed_ms);
   479	            printf("Performance:\n");
   480	            printf("  Time per iteration: %.4f ms\n", time_per_iteration);
   481	            printf("  Elements/sec: %.4f GigaElements/s\n", giga_elements_per_second);
   482	            printf("  Performance: %.4f GFLOPS\n", giga_elements_per_second * 22.0);
   483	            printf("  Result hash: %016llX\n\n", static_cast<unsigned long long>(global_hash));
   484	        }
   485	
   486	        if (print_results_requested) {
   487	            std::vector<int> receive_counts;
   488	            std::vector<int> displacements;
   489	            std::vector<val_t> global_energy;
   490	            if (rank == 0) {
   491	                receive_counts.resize(ranks);
   492	                displacements.resize(ranks);
   493	                for (int process = 0; process < ranks; ++process) {
   494	                    const RowPartition process_partition = partitionRows(grid_size, process, ranks);
   495	                    receive_counts[process] = process_partition.rows * grid_size;
   496	                    displacements[process] = process_partition.first_row * grid_size;
   497	                }
   498	                global_energy.resize(global_elements);
   499	            }
   500	            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Gatherv(host_energy.data(), static_cast<int>(local_elements), MPI_DOUBLE,
   501	                                  rank == 0 ? global_energy.data() : nullptr,
   502	                                  rank == 0 ? receive_counts.data() : nullptr,
   503	                                  rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
   504	                                  MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   505	            if (rank == 0) {
   506	                print_results(global_energy, "ElementEnergy");
   507	            }
   508	        }
   509	
   510	        if (validate && !validateResults(host_energy, host_flux, rank)) {
   511	            exit_code = 1;
   512	        }
   513	    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
   514	        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, error.what());
   515	        exit_code = 1;
   516	    }
   517	
   518	    if (copy_stream != nullptr) cudaStreamDestroy(copy_stream);
   519	    if (boundary_stream != nullptr) cudaStreamDestroy(boundary_stream);
   520	    if (compute_stream != nullptr) cudaStreamDestroy(compute_stream);

     1	cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.24)
     2	project(unstructured_benchmark LANGUAGES CXX CUDA)
     3	
     4	set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
     5	set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
     6	set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD 20)
     7	set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
     8	
     9	# Build native SASS by default for the accelerator on which the benchmark is
    10	# configured.  This target-level setting deliberately overrides CMake's often
    11	# conservative compiler default.  Sites cross-compiling can pass, for example,
    12	# -DUNSTRUCTURED_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES=80-real instead.
    13	set(UNSTRUCTURED_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native CACHE STRING
    14	    "CUDA architectures for the unstructured benchmark")
    15	
    16	find_package(MPI REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)
    17	find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)
    18	find_package(CUDAToolkit REQUIRED)
    19	
    20	add_executable(unstructured unstructured.cpp)
    21	set_source_files_properties(unstructured.cpp PROPERTIES LANGUAGE CUDA)
    22	
    23	set_target_properties(unstructured PROPERTIES
    24	    CUDA_SEPARABLE_COMPILATION OFF
    25	    CUDA_RESOLVE_DEVICE_SYMBOLS OFF
    26	    CUDA_ARCHITECTURES "${UNSTRUCTURED_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES}"
    27	)
    28	
    29	# CUDA owns this translation unit, so forward CPU optimization and OpenMP flags
    30	# explicitly to its host compiler as well as optimizing device code.
    31	target_compile_options(unstructured PRIVATE
    32	    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-O3;-lineinfo;-Xcompiler=-O3,-march=native,-Wall,-Wextra,-fopenmp>
    33	)
    34	
    35	target_link_libraries(unstructured PRIVATE
    36	    MPI::MPI_CXX
    37	    OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX
    38	    CUDA::cudart
    39	    m
    40	)

+void launchRows(const val_t* energy,
+                const val_t* accumulated_flux,
+                val_t* next_energy,
+                val_t* next_accumulated_flux,

+    updateRowsKernel<<<blocks, kThreadsPerBlock, 0, stream>>>(
+        energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux, top_halo, bottom_halo,

-uint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {

+void runSimulation(const RowPartition& partition,
+                   int grid_size,
+                   int iterations,
+                   int rank,
+                   int ranks,
+                   val_t*& energy,
+                   val_t*& accumulated_flux,
+                   val_t*& next_energy,
+                   val_t*& next_accumulated_flux,
+                   val_t* top_halo,
+                   val_t* bottom_halo,
+                   val_t* send_top,
+                   val_t* send_bottom,
+                   val_t* receive_top,
+                   val_t* receive_bottom,
+                   cudaStream_t compute_stream,
+                   cudaStream_t boundary_stream,
+                   cudaStream_t copy_stream) {
+    const int previous_rank = rank == 0 ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank - 1;
+    const int next_rank = rank + 1 == ranks ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank + 1;
+    const size_t row_bytes = static_cast<size_t>(grid_size) * sizeof(val_t);
+
+    for (int iteration = 0; iteration < iterations; ++iteration) {
+        // Begin the host-staged halo exchange.  Pinned buffers make this work with
+        // both CUDA-aware and conventional MPI implementations without a runtime
+        // dependency on a particular MPI GPU transport.
+        if (previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(send_top, energy, row_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, copy_stream));
+        }
+        if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(send_bottom,
+                                       energy + static_cast<size_t>(partition.rows - 1) * grid_size,
+                                       row_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, copy_stream));
+        }
+
+        // Interior rows do not depend on a halo and run while MPI transfers edges.
+        launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
+                   top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition, 1, partition.rows - 1,
+                   compute_stream);
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(copy_stream));
+
+        MPI_Request requests[4];
+        int request_count = 0;
+        if (previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Irecv(receive_top, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, previous_rank, kBottomHaloTag,
+                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Isend(send_top, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, previous_rank, kTopHaloTag,
+                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
+        }
+        if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Irecv(receive_bottom, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, next_rank, kTopHaloTag,
+                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Isend(send_bottom, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, next_rank, kBottomHaloTag,
+                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
+        }
+        if (request_count != 0) {
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Waitall(request_count, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE));
+        }
+
+        if (previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(top_halo, receive_top, row_bytes,
+                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, copy_stream));
+        }
+        if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(bottom_halo, receive_bottom, row_bytes,
+                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, copy_stream));
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(copy_stream));
+
+        // The boundary stream writes disjoint rows, so it overlaps the remaining
+        // interior work.  A one-row partition has one boundary row, not two.
+        if (partition.rows == 1) {
+            launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
+                       top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition, 0, 1, boundary_stream);
+        } else {
+            launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
+                       top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition, 0, 1, boundary_stream);
+            launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
+                       top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition,
+                       partition.rows - 1, partition.rows, boundary_stream);
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(compute_stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(boundary_stream));
+        std::swap(energy, next_energy);
+        std::swap(accumulated_flux, next_accumulated_flux);
     }
-    return hash;
 }

-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
+uint64_t computeLocalHash(const std::vector<val_t>& energy,
+                          const std::vector<val_t>& accumulated_flux,
+                          idx_t global_offset) {
+    uint64_t local_hash = 0;
+#pragma omp parallel for reduction(^ : local_hash) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < energy.size(); ++i) {
+        const idx_t global_index = global_offset + i;
+        const uint64_t energy_bits = std::bit_cast<uint64_t>(energy[i]);
+        const uint64_t flux_bits = std::bit_cast<uint64_t>(accumulated_flux[i]);
+        local_hash ^= (energy_bits + global_index) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        local_hash ^= (flux_bits + global_index) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+    }
+    return local_hash;
+}
+
+bool validateResults(const std::vector<val_t>& energy,
+                     const std::vector<val_t>& accumulated_flux,
+                     int rank) {
+    val_t local_energy_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t local_flux_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t local_energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
+    val_t local_energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
+
+#pragma omp parallel for reduction(+ : local_energy_sum, local_flux_sum) \
+    reduction(max : local_energy_max) reduction(min : local_energy_min) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < energy.size(); ++i) {
+        local_energy_sum += energy[i];
+        local_flux_sum += accumulated_flux[i];
+        local_energy_max = std::max(local_energy_max, energy[i]);
+        local_energy_min = std::min(local_energy_min, energy[i]);
+    }
+
+    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t energy_max = 0.0;
+    val_t energy_min = 0.0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_sum, &energy_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_flux_sum, &flux_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_max, &energy_max, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_min, &energy_min, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MIN, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+
+    int valid = 1;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        printf("Validation results:\n");
+        printf("  Energy sum: %.12f\n", energy_sum);
+        printf("  Flux sum: %.2f\n", flux_sum);
+        printf("  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", energy_min, energy_max);
+
+        constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {
+            printf("  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        } else if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {
+            printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {
+            printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
+            printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (valid != 0) {
+            printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    return valid != 0;
+}
+

+    if (compute_stream != nullptr) cudaStreamDestroy(compute_stream);
+    if (receive_bottom != nullptr) cudaFreeHost(receive_bottom);
+    if (receive_top != nullptr) cudaFreeHost(receive_top);
+    if (send_bottom != nullptr) cudaFreeHost(send_bottom);

+    if (local_comm != MPI_COMM_NULL) MPI_Comm_free(&local_comm);

+    MPI_Allreduce(&exit_code, &global_exit_code, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return global_exit_code;

+void launchRows(const val_t* energy,
+                const val_t* accumulated_flux,
+                val_t* next_energy,
+                val_t* next_accumulated_flux,
+                const val_t* top_halo,
+                const val_t* bottom_halo,
+                int grid_size,
+                const RowPartition& partition,

     }
-    
-    printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
-    return true;
+
+    const size_t elements = static_cast<size_t>(end_local_row - first_local_row) * grid_size;
+    const int blocks = static_cast<int>((elements + kThreadsPerBlock - 1) / kThreadsPerBlock);
+    updateRowsKernel<<<blocks, kThreadsPerBlock, 0, stream>>>(
+        energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux, top_halo, bottom_halo,
+        grid_size, partition.first_row, partition.rows, first_local_row, end_local_row);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());

-// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification
-uint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {
-    uint64_t hash = 0;
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {
-        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values
-        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);
-        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);
-        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
-        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+void runSimulation(const RowPartition& partition,
+                   int grid_size,
+                   int iterations,
+                   int rank,
+                   int ranks,
+                   val_t*& energy,
+                   val_t*& accumulated_flux,
+                   val_t*& next_energy,
+                   val_t*& next_accumulated_flux,
+                   val_t* top_halo,
+                   val_t* bottom_halo,
+                   val_t* send_top,
+                   val_t* send_bottom,
+                   val_t* receive_top,
+                   val_t* receive_bottom,
+                   cudaStream_t compute_stream,
+                   cudaStream_t boundary_stream,
+                   cudaStream_t copy_stream) {
+    const int previous_rank = rank == 0 ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank - 1;
+    const int next_rank = rank + 1 == ranks ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank + 1;
+    const size_t row_bytes = static_cast<size_t>(grid_size) * sizeof(val_t);
+
+    for (int iteration = 0; iteration < iterations; ++iteration) {
+        // Begin the host-staged halo exchange.  Pinned buffers make this work with
+        // both CUDA-aware and conventional MPI implementations without a runtime
+        // dependency on a particular MPI GPU transport.
+        if (previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(send_top, energy, row_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, copy_stream));
+        }
+        if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(send_bottom,
+                                       energy + static_cast<size_t>(partition.rows - 1) * grid_size,
+                                       row_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, copy_stream));
+        }
+
+        // Interior rows do not depend on a halo and run while MPI transfers edges.
+        launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
+                   top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition, 1, partition.rows - 1,
+                   compute_stream);
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(copy_stream));
+
+        MPI_Request requests[4];
+        int request_count = 0;
+        if (previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Irecv(receive_top, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, previous_rank, kBottomHaloTag,
+                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Isend(send_top, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, previous_rank, kTopHaloTag,
+                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
+        }
+        if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Irecv(receive_bottom, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, next_rank, kTopHaloTag,
+                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Isend(send_bottom, grid_size, MPI_DOUBLE, next_rank, kBottomHaloTag,
+                                MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]));
+        }
+        if (request_count != 0) {
+            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Waitall(request_count, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE));
+        }
+
+        if (previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(top_halo, receive_top, row_bytes,
+                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, copy_stream));
+        }
+        if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(bottom_halo, receive_bottom, row_bytes,
+                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, copy_stream));
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(copy_stream));
+
+        // The boundary stream writes disjoint rows, so it overlaps the remaining
+        // interior work.  A one-row partition has one boundary row, not two.
+        if (partition.rows == 1) {
+            launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
+                       top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition, 0, 1, boundary_stream);
+        } else {
+            launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
+                       top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition, 0, 1, boundary_stream);
+            launchRows(energy, accumulated_flux, next_energy, next_accumulated_flux,
+                       top_halo, bottom_halo, grid_size, partition,
+                       partition.rows - 1, partition.rows, boundary_stream);
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(compute_stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(boundary_stream));
+        std::swap(energy, next_energy);
+        std::swap(accumulated_flux, next_accumulated_flux);
     }
-    return hash;
 }
 
-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
+uint64_t computeLocalHash(const std::vector<val_t>& energy,
+                          const std::vector<val_t>& accumulated_flux,
+                          idx_t global_offset) {
+    uint64_t local_hash = 0;
+#pragma omp parallel for reduction(^ : local_hash) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < energy.size(); ++i) {
+        const idx_t global_index = global_offset + i;
+        const uint64_t energy_bits = std::bit_cast<uint64_t>(energy[i]);
+        const uint64_t flux_bits = std::bit_cast<uint64_t>(accumulated_flux[i]);
+        local_hash ^= (energy_bits + global_index) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        local_hash ^= (flux_bits + global_index) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
+    }
+    return local_hash;
+}
+
+bool validateResults(const std::vector<val_t>& energy,
+                     const std::vector<val_t>& accumulated_flux,
+                     int rank) {
+    val_t local_energy_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t local_flux_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t local_energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
+    val_t local_energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
+
+#pragma omp parallel for reduction(+ : local_energy_sum, local_flux_sum) \
+    reduction(max : local_energy_max) reduction(min : local_energy_min) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < energy.size(); ++i) {
+        local_energy_sum += energy[i];
+        local_flux_sum += accumulated_flux[i];
+        local_energy_max = std::max(local_energy_max, energy[i]);
+        local_energy_min = std::min(local_energy_min, energy[i]);
+    }
+
+    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t energy_max = 0.0;
+    val_t energy_min = 0.0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_sum, &energy_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_flux_sum, &flux_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_max, &energy_max, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_min, &energy_min, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MIN, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+
+    int valid = 1;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        printf("Validation results:\n");
+        printf("  Energy sum: %.12f\n", energy_sum);
+        printf("  Flux sum: %.2f\n", flux_sum);
+        printf("  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", energy_min, energy_max);
+
+        constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {
+            printf("  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        } else if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {
+            printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {
+            printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
+            printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (valid != 0) {
+            printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    return valid != 0;
+}
